- **agent-data-monitor**: Monitor website data and run recurring workflows with agent-data APIs. Use for price or availability tracking, job alerts, social listening, competitive intelligence, research digests, and other requests to watch for changes or send recurring reports—even without mentioning agent-data. Owns source selection, retrieval state, change detection, scheduling, and delivery. Uses agent-data-search when it needs a data source. ([agent-data/skills](https://github.com/agent-data/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-data-monitor) · ★1)
